# Master Roadmap — Multi-Sport Player Prop Research Platform

## 2026-11-27 — NHL public-only qualification pilot

- [ ] **[PF-48]** Verify the public-only PrizePicks NHL Standard full-game shots-on-goal pilot end to end. Use the authorized immutable PickFinder capture, confirm an exact two-sided match against the unmetered PrizePicks partner endpoint, and independently validate official NHL schedule, roster, prior history and final boxscore. Preserve the five-minute source clock and a fifteen-minute pregame lock. Freeze one threshold per player/game, then compare with always Higher and always Lower on identical offers, report calibration and game-weighted results, and require explicit qualification before any production or Discord suggestion use. No private checkout, raw partner payload publication, paid requests, native push requirement, or allowlist change. Shadow infrastructure does not count as live model acceptance.

- **PF-48 implementation and live intake verified, qualification open.** [PR #466](https://github.com/Thedarkwillow/nhl-betting-platform1/pull/466) merged at `2c5ebc98` after 13 successful hosted checks and one expected skip. The [first main run](https://github.com/Thedarkwillow/nhl-betting-platform1/actions/runs/46085067164) shipped its public status with zero forecasts and grades. The latest archived PickFinder NHL PrizePicks board was stale at that run. Its immutable offer part independently shows 0/57 half-point SOG side observations with an explicit start time. Separately, a live PrizePicks partner API read at 3:53 p.m. Pacific found 21 unambiguous two-sided Standard full-game SOG scopes, with no paid requests or private checkout. These API scopes are not qualified forecasts. Next evidence is a fresh exact PickFinder game-start match, a pregame freeze, and a prospective independent final grade.

- **PF-48 game-clock handoff.** Admit an absent board-row start only after replaying the independently controlled three-zone receipt bundle for the same PickFinder event against its official NHL game. Preserve source observation time, offer scope, exact roster, freshness and pregame locks. Reject contradictory or late proof. The [fresh live capture](https://github.com/Thedarkwillow/nhl-betting-platform1/actions/runs/46086680129) and [initial pilot](https://github.com/Thedarkwillow/nhl-betting-platform1/actions/runs/46087384594) reproduce the issue before the handoff. Same-clock replay of their immutable NHL board recovered six sides across three Seattle–Edmonton lines using the exact official event ID. This replay wrote zero forecasts. Still requires a genuinely future immutable forecast and independent grade.

- [ ] **[PF-49]** Qualify probabilities separately for each supported NHL, NFL and NBA prop market, book, tier, period, probability target, line class and versioned model. First eligible public archive lanes are the NHL Standard full-game shots-on-goal pilot, verified NHL/NFL forward forecasts, and independently graded NBA full-game forecasts. Use only immutable, genuinely pregame originals and independently committed finals, exclude resolved pushes from binary calibration and reject conflicting duplicates, and commit each past-only fit before scoring later observations. Review game-weighted Brier, log loss, reliability, uncertainty and always-Higher/always-Lower on identical decisions. Require raw-model and neutral-50% improvement for probability review. The Nov 23–25 Standard chronological replay had 289 decisions with Brier 0.2626 raw, 0.2505 tier prior, and 0.2500 neutral. No promotion. Further targets and markets remain unqualified until their own exact evidence is collected, including when there is no adapter. No auto-promotion, paid API usage, or production/Discord probability changes.

## 2026-11-27 Pacific — PickFinder completion work

- **PF-16 current scope.** As of this sprint the authored roadmap contains 49 PickFinder task IDs, 25 checked and 24 open. Continue the live browser inventory and analytics/archive work, close delivery work only with actual channel receipts, and separate genuinely future original-platform forecasts and calibration from historical replay. Native iPhone pushes are excluded under PF-19 and remain out of scope.
- **PF-06 scoped recovery.** The [Nov 27 afternoon manual board run](https://github.com/Thedarkwillow/nhl-betting-platform1/actions/runs/46086680129) archived 27 of 28 requested partitions, including six populated traversals for NBA, NHL and NFL. It recorded 17 populated, five empty, four disabled and one partial partition. Dota 2 PrizePicks had no checkpoint, and tennis PrizePicks stopped at a partial first page. The new manual recovery mode attempts only explicitly named partitions, with the same protected windows, authentication, archive and paid-request rules. Initial targeted recovery acceptance remains open.
- **PF-22 paired benchmark implementation.** The manual-only benchmark pins a single immutable board and a deterministic cohort of four distinct full-game player routes, then measures one/two/two/one tabs against the same ordered cohort and settings. Aggregate evidence distinguishes navigation, normalized bytes, feature reads, wall-clock time and serialized aggregate checkpoint time. Requires route and field-shape parity and independently verified receipts. No claim of actual production archival speed, unlimited throughput, or change to scheduled workers. Live paired measurement and recommendation remain pending until the hosted readback.

## 2026-11-27 — PickFinder engine comparison verified

- **PF-21 completed for the browser-engine comparison.** [PR #463](https://github.com/Thedarkwillow/nhl-betting-platform1/pull/463) merged at `aeb53488` after three hosted checks passed and one expected skip. In the [explicitly dispatched main run](https://github.com/Thedarkwillow/nhl-betting-platform1/actions/runs/46078838691), both engines matched on the synthetic fixture. Keep Playwright; no Selenium promotion or failover, no proof of full coverage or independent session parity, and no raw session data or new paid use. PF-22 still requires the controlled one-vs-two worker experiment.

## 2026-11-27 — Full PrizePicksAPI NBA, NHL and NFL prop boards

- [ ] **[PP-BOARD-01]** Collect the current PrizePicksAPI board separately for NBA, NHL and NFL, with all active markets, periods and rungs, including Standard, Goblin and Demon where offered. Request compact pages up to 1,000 rows and follow each cursor to its explicit end. Require a single fresh provider observation per league and reject duplicate IDs, wrong-league rows, stale/partial envelopes or missing pagination links. The pinned full private NHL/NFL/MLB tests, selective market policies, official-grade reconstruction and no-release assertions **passed** in [run 44225492935](https://github.com/Thedarkwillow/nhl-betting-platform1/actions/runs/44225492935), followed by new original-board chronology validation in [run 44226039040](https://github.com/Thedarkwillow/nhl-betting-platform1/actions/runs/44226039040). The new post-merge full regression [run 44226237917](https://github.com/Thedarkwillow/nhl-betting-platform1/actions/runs/44226237917) also **succeeded**; old frozen picks remain excluded from the newly preregistered cohorts. Existing auto NHL opportunity, NFL original future freeze and NFL grading workflows passed after the main merge of the new models, while the stricter chronology patch had its own new post-merge validation dispatched.

**Root question: improve Standard accuracy vs plain strategies by refusing weak props, not guessing sides.** Five requested engineering priorities:
1. **Market error diagnosis:** keep the distinct frozen NHL Standard future game/Pacific-slate and NFL first-threshold by-market/selected-side comparisons against the original same-line always-Higher/always-Lower. Old cohort results currently trail always-Lower (NHL 53.0% vs 55.1% on 575 decided/9 slates; NFL 46.3% vs 52.0% on 1,289 thresholds/14 games). NFL 2025 historical opponent matchup model was not consistently better than basic MAE for receiving/rushing. These are diagnosis, not posthoc automatic overrides.
2. **Opportunity-based NHL model research:** the existing opponent-volume, prior-ice-time, original shots/points, role and power-play exposure challenger now freezes explicit candidate evidence with an optional *new* precommitted selection-abstention policy. Require >=12 prior appearances, >=8 opponent games, strong prior TOI (at least 700 seconds SOG / 800 points), a recent player appearance, and original Higher/Lower agreement among fixed empirical, opportunity and position forecast signals. Points also require exact historical PP seconds and adequate verified PP-point component rows. Insufficient information becomes an abstention, not a substitute zero or invented source.
3. **Position/market-specific NFL policy:** original v2 strict injury/offer checks stay intact. A new fixed NFL Standard market margin and last-five usage agreement shadow covers suitable passing/rushing/receiving markets with >=12 prior games, without changing original directions or pretending current snap shares are known. Kicker/combo props are **not** included in this new shadow until their specialist opportunity and exact original market contract are independently verified. Existing separate kicking research remains intact.
4. **Abstain rather than force:** new annotations are strictly research-only, never champion replacement, never a new offered opposite side, and never released to the website as bettable calibrated probabilities. NHL shadow attaches **only if both the original board generation and first Git commit were after the newly introduced policy's first Git commit**, with the derived shadow frozen before event start. NFL likewise requires its own policy first Git commit before the original board and before kickoff.
5. **Untouched future comparison and grading:** existing archived original shots/points and NFL future-only original side validators now compute the predeclared selective cohort's decided/pending counts, game/date diversity, Brier diagnostics for NHL, and identical-threshold directional baselines (with game-cluster uncertainty for NFL). These require >=30 independent games and >=20 distinct dates/slates for initial evaluation. This preliminary gate alone never establishes payout EV, calibration or statistically significant profits.

## Status vocabulary
- **Built:** Code or data exists, but may not yet meet production acceptance criteria.
- **Running:** GitHub Action has a cron and recent successful run; individual future executions still need monitoring.
- **Partial:** Some supporting evidence exists but completeness, accuracy or lawful reuse has not been established.
- **Blocked/deferred:** Critical source, validation, contract or feature not yet available.
- **Never equate:** Historical sportsbook/pick'em provider lines ≠ original first-party platform offers; boxscore direction ≠ official settled DNP/Reboot result; retrospective win rate ≠ prospectively saved model accuracy; indicative API American odds ≠ actual PrizePicks multiplier; marginal hit probabilities ≠ joint slip probability or EV.

## Roadmap operating agreement — this GitHub file is the source of truth

- **Every actionable checkbox has a stable task ID.** `[ ]` = incomplete; `[x]` = implemented/verified to its specifically stated scope. A green CI check or script commit alone is not sufficient to mark a larger production capability finished.
- **When we finish a roadmap task**, update its existing checkbox **in this file in the same GitHub change**, add its task ID, completion date, commit/workflow URL and acceptance evidence to the `Verified completion log` section below, and confirm its specific gate passed. If acceptance is partial, **leave unchecked** and update progress notes instead. Never check it off just because scheduled work is pending.
- Grandfathered checkmarks from the existing roadmap are **implemented infrastructure milestones**, not verified profitability, production certification, or fully complete phases; their initial evidence and limitations are listed in the completion log. Do not silently upgrade their claims.
- Every new implementation should reference at least one stable task ID in its GitHub commit/PR description or accompanying completion log.
- **Cross-source reuse:** record progress against every relevant existing task ID, regardless of provider; maintain the [shared evidence index](#cross-source-evidence-index--2026-11-25). Reuse verified artifacts; do not duplicate collection for overlapping requirements or mark a parent complete from a scoped child milestone.
- Priorities: **P0** = trustworthy predictive record, data licensing, money/security controls; **P1** = high-value differentiators and subscription user experience; **P2** = advanced premium features and growth experiments.
- Use objective gates: test run, audited source sample, locked pregame predictions, observed latency, calibration and reliability, successful end-to-end phone workflow, and written license/payment approvals where applicable.
- **No payments or automated wagers** are authorized by a roadmap checkbox. Subscriptions require an explicit later go-live decision, platform/provider approval and legal review; wagers always require explicit user authorization.

**Already completed scoped tasks reinforced, not re-completed:** P01-04 (source/derived separation), P05-01 (structured source tier), P08-01 (canonical entities), ESP-072 (archive readback), ESP-073 (public checkpoint observability). Existing checkmarks preserved. Other legacy completed tasks are not newly credited to PickFinder.

**Phase review:** P01–03, P05–11, SHARP, EDGE, UX, OPS, TRUST, MLBX, CHAT and Phase 22 receive the scoped cross-references above. Phase 4 projection work uses independent historical evidence separately from PickFinder inputs; Phase 10/23 expansion gets observed coverage, not a new sport launch. No newly verified change to payment/commerce (Phase 15 beyond BIZ-01), growth (Phase 18), original MLB repository (Phase 19), or earlier verified NHL milestones (Phase 21). No broader product/security/settlement gates waived.

**Deduplication policy:** canonical evidence lanes E1–E9 own collection/reconciliation; consuming roadmap IDs reuse the same artifacts. Keep overlapping IDs until their distinct acceptance criteria pass. For future work, identify existing evidence and task IDs before creating collectors or adding subscriptions; update all affected original tasks and scoped completion log together. Priority order: source context and exact offer/tier evidence → identity and odds joins → deeper profiles/dashboard and season-aware captures → frozen future records and independent grading → qualified website integration. Do not confuse a successful CI run with live acceptance.

## The product moat — why a subscriber would pay

A subscriber should get **timely, reproducible, understandable research** unavailable from a basic hit-rate table: source-labelled and freshness-checked PrizePicks/Underdog comparisons, uncertainty-aware probabilities against sharp-market baselines, goalie/starter/injury effects, alternate-line curves, realistic correlation-aware 2–6-pick simulation, alerts when assumptions or offers change, a frozen historical forecast ledger, and honest evidence of both wins and misses. The strongest product claim is **auditable decision quality**, not guaranteed winning picks.

Current competitors already advertise hit-rate filters, odds movement, injuries, +EV indicators and alerts. These are the **competitive baseline**, not our unique advantage; our differentiation must be verified correctness, source transparency, probability calibration, protected costs and useful mobile workflows.

## Market research and licensing anchors

- [The Odds API terms](https://the-odds-api.com/terms-and-conditions.html): commercial user-facing dashboards, research and derived analytics are permitted; standalone resale/repackaging/redistribution as a raw data feed is prohibited. This is **provider-specific**, not permission to redistribute PrizePicks, Underdog, SportsGameOdds, or independent aggregator feeds. Verify the full paid offering and public exports separately.
- [Outlier prop finder](https://help.outlier.bet/en/articles/6711738-research-player-propositions-with-outlier-s-prop-finder) and [Outlier features](https://www.outlier.bet/): benchmark features include recent hit-rate filters, market comparisons, movements, injury context and alerts; avoid unsupported claims that our site is more accurate.
- [Stripe restricted-business terms](https://stripe.com/legal/restricted-businesses): wagering-adjacent payments may require review; **do not assume payment processor approval** just because we sell research access.
- [FTC subscription guidance](https://consumer.ftc.gov/articles/getting-and-out-free-trials-auto-renewals-and-negative-option-subscriptions): communicate price, renewal, trial, consent and cancellation clearly. Note that legal rules changed; verify applicable federal/state law at launch, not a superseded version.
- [OWASP API Top 10](https://devguide.owasp.org/en/07-training-education/07-api-top-ten/): authorization, authentication, abuse limits, rate limiting, unsafe third-party inputs and operational security should be launch gates.

**2026-11-08 EDGE-04 / P03-06 / P03-09 / EDGE-12 new benchmark design (PARTIAL, no edge claim):**
Audited the 187 original frozen model-selected NHL props (126/186 decided correct, one push, five games). 116 Goblin and 41 Demon were all Higher-only; **Standard-only** had 30 total (29 decided), **13 model-correct vs 15 simulated always-Higher on those same Standard thresholds**. The combined 67.7% selected-direction rate should **not** be used to claim a strategic advantage over always Higher; the Standard model score was only 44.8% vs 51.7% for Higher in this tiny correlated sample. Added a private, fail-closed same-threshold paired comparison with game-cluster bootstrap, cohort integrity, tier separation, future-only pre-policy proof, a **fixed February 1, 2027 evaluation horizon** and no model/wager/payout promotion. Primary comparison requires **>=200 future decided Standard props, >=30 completed NHL games, >=30 actual Lower selections**, with a positive game-cluster lower bound only considered after the locked window; this is a **minimum evidence gate, not guaranteed statistical power**. Verify original available sides, equal full-board selection rules, authentic settlements and payout-EV separately before any claim of a realizable edge. [Frozen benchmark protocol](NHL_LOCKED_STANDARD_VS_HIGHER_BENCHMARK_2026-11-08.md). **Keep P03-06/P03-09/EDGE-04/12 unchecked.**

**2026-11-08 model challenger + freeze-recovery sprint (P03-06/P03-09/EDGE-04; P01-05, PARTIAL):**
Created a **future-only**, first-Git-commit-frozen Standard last-5, last-10 and agreement-only (+0.5-count margin) shadow strategy for already chosen original model prop lines. All forecasts and abstentions originate in the exact original pregame selected-pick snapshot, with prior-date official final history only; official NHL results are graded later on the same lines alongside the unchanged champion and hypothetical Higher/Lower, split by strategy coverage and NHL games. Original Nov 5–6 data are not presented as prospective challenger results. Added a **no-additional-paid-source** workflow after 6 AM scheduled collection: if a verified original-model precommit already exists, skip; otherwise try once from a same-day, complete 06 AM-purpose third-party board at most 25 minutes old and original cached NHL history; prove the new immutable Git commit before puck drop, or raise a date-specific private issue and fail visibly. Never scrape live PrizePicks during recovery. [Technical evidence](NHL_FROZEN_SIMPLE_STRATEGY_CHALLENGE_2026-11-08.md). **Do not check off P03-06, EDGE-04/12 or daily 6 AM reliability until real on-time runs, future outcome samples, calibration and independent platform settlement are verified.**

### November 6, 2026 — NHL market-specific projection challenger (partial, not promoted)

**P03-07 / P03-08 / P03-09 / Phase 4 / EDGE-03 / EDGE-05:** Implemented a new privately archived read-only `nhl-market-trained-v3-shadow` projection alongside the unchanged `weighted-form-v1` production champion. Per-market formula choice is derived exclusively from 2024–25 official NHL training MAE (minimum 1,000 rows, 100 distinct games and 30 dates); historical 2025–26 results are descriptive comparison only. Selected methods: prior mean for shots/assists/points; stable blend for goals; **abstain** for blocked shots and goalie saves because that 2024–25 archive has no qualifying training observations for those markets. New regression tests enforce train/holdout separation and past-date-only projections; GitHub workflow archives separate private daily shadow without overwriting existing frozen snapshots. The former 2025–26 holdout was already examined during model design, so its lower historical MAE does not prove a new uncontaminated accuracy edge. No first-party fantasy-platform settlement, verified EV, calibrated Higher/Lower rate, model release or new roadmap checkbox is claimed. [Method and acceptance gates](NHL_MARKET_TRAINED_V3_SHADOW_2026-11-06.md).

**Additional partial November 6, 2026 — same-original-line future evidence:** The market-trained v3 shadow is now attached ONLY to newly captured, unchanged, original-source NHL full-game offers in the first-write distribution snapshot with training-policy hash and a true pregame timestamp gate. An independent, no-paid-calls scorer compares **champion vs v3** on the SAME common Higher event after official NHL FINAL, with duplicate Higher/Lower offered-side suppression, pending/push separation, original Git commit-time proof and per-game uncertainty safeguards. The scorer will not backfill v3 into pre-existing frozen originals or count offered directions as optimizer picks. No newly decided prospective v3 sample exists yet; no calibrated probability, profit, model promotion or milestone completion is inferred. [Freeze/grade contract](NHL_MARKET_TRAINED_V3_SHADOW_2026-11-06.md).

### November 6, 2026 — user-prioritized four NHL markets (partial)

**First:** Hits, Time on Ice, Faceoffs Won, and Goalie Fantasy Score. Code now recognizes all four as research-only canonical markets while denying live rankings/lineups until original provider rules and stats are validated. New official NHL final gamecenter stat fields and bounded exact Stats REST faceoff-win supplement, past-date-only 8-sample exploratory projections, private `outputs/projections/nhl-four-priority-shadow-latest.json` output and unit tests are implemented. Full-game NHL TOI can include OT while Underdog's TOI rule excludes OT. Underdog's goalie fantasy **6 win / +0.6 save / −3 goal allowed** scoring is separately versioned and must not be presented as PrizePicks scoring. **PrizePicks NHL Goalie Fantasy Score formula is NOT verified** on an official public page; the authoritative original app Scoring Chart must be obtained before any fantasy scoring/pick grading. No claimed launch, official first-party line, verified probability, payout EV or hit rate. Repeated complete-date re-archives are bounded to eight dates per official stats catch-up job. [Evidence and remaining acceptance gates](NHL_FOUR_PRIORITY_MARKETS_2026-11-06.md).

**Follow-up November 6, 2026 — PrizePicks NHL Goalie Fantasy user-provided scoring rule (partial, implemented):** Added a separate private research formula `10*official_win + 10*official_shutout + 0.3*saves - 2*goals_against` from the user's supplied values, explicitly **not independently verified against the PrizePicks in-app chart**. Added exact official NHL per-game goalie/summary `wins` and `shutouts` records, strict player/game/start/goal-against/save cross-checks and null-on-conflict guards. A goalie who starts does not necessarily get credited with the individual win; zero GA is not enough to credit an official shutout. New `prizePicksGoalieFantasyScore` historical raw stat and eight-game private exploratory projections are not original PrizePicks settlements, calibrated probabilities, live picks or EV. Separate Underdog goalie fantasy coefficients and all six production NHL model categories stay unchanged. Official result archive schema 4 will incrementally refresh up to eight dates per free NHL stats run; independent in-app PrizePicks scoring verification, goalie participation/settlement rules, richer history and prospective evidence remain required. [Evidence, formula and gates](NHL_FOUR_PRIORITY_MARKETS_2026-11-06.md).

**November 6 further expansion (partial, implementation, NOT production):** The official source pipeline now collects skater **Power Play Points** (exact NHL Stats REST `ppPoints`), signed **Plus/Minus**, and goalie **Goals Allowed** with exact NHL player/game/source integrity; standalone 8-prior-sample projections are archived privately in `nhl-extra-market-shadow-latest.json`. Four original new research-market shadows remain separate. The free board research analyzer supports **conditional automatic Higher/Lower *research ranking*** for Hits, Faceoffs Won, Power Play Points, Plus/Minus, Goalie Goals Allowed, PrizePicks Goalie Fantasy and Time on Ice *only when precise line/unit/overtime/source identity and original pre-offer shadow evidence exist*. The existing 2–6 pick optimizer may include eligible Standard-tier independent-player research picks, but never claims calibrated hit probability, complete correlations, expected value, or betting-readiness. Goalie risk/unknown starter blocks goalie lineup inclusion. This is not a release to place bets.

**Further scoped acceptance: separate forward-grade cohort.** The existing Git-first-commit NHL lineup grader now partitions exact original-player experimental market selections into their own official-result scoring cohort, with signed plus-minus, precisely minutes-converted full-game TOI and the versioned user-provided PrizePicks goalie formula. It refuses unresolved underlying stats and never mixes experimental outcomes into the established six-market champion hit rate. It still does NOT verify platform settlement, nor does it automatically promote or validate uncalibrated new-market model probabilities; a separate standalone experimental pick policy and stronger out-of-sample tests remain open. [Detailed evidence](NHL_ALL_MARKETS_EXPANSION_2026-11-06.md).

**Composite and period status (partial identity scaffolding, NOT selectable):** SOG (Combo), Goalie Saves (Combo), Goals (Combo) now have canonical names plus strict official participant+game identity and same-game historical sample audit. Actual provider member IDs, validated joint models, settlements and safe combo-aware lineup grading are unavailable, so these cannot yet become automatic picks. 1P/2P/3P markets are tracked with strict period source/settlement barriers but currently lack official per-period historical collection and remain unavailable for picks. No historical data or synthetic "successes" can override this. The canonical registry now has 16 full-game market names (6 original + 4 initial user-priority + 3 extra individual + 3 combos), with period-specific variants separately audited. The existing checklist remains unchecked until acceptance gates. [Full readiness and integration evidence](NHL_ALL_MARKETS_EXPANSION_2026-11-06.md).



### November 6–7, 2026 — free official period-game source pipeline and structured combo audits (partial)

**Implemented and under CI:** dedicated NHL official `/gamecenter/{id}/play-by-play` + fully paginated `/shiftcharts` collector for **REG periods 1–3**, producing individual-player SOG/goals/assists/points/blocks/hits/faceoffs and goalie saves/goals-allowed with exact shifts/period participation. Every market compares its REG+OT reconstructed total to the original complete official boxscore; discrepancies become explicit `null` quarantines, not invented period outcomes. Standalone scheduled GitHub workflow `collect-nhl-period-events.yml` archives at most two complete game dates per run, first verifying final-game snapshot provenance, and rebuilds isolated `outputs/period-stats/history.json`. Previously period-only placeholder audits now recognize actual official player/market period samples without pretending they are platform settled.

**Combo evidence:** structured original provider member-player ID, exact future game ID for **every** member, role, sum operator, source timestamp and event-period contract. Same-game vs cross-game combo requests are explicitly distinguished; no inferred members from display labels, fabricated historical paired games, shared-game correlation coefficient or payout. `src/nhl-combo-source-evidence.cjs` and tests refuse incomplete metadata.

**November 6–7 live validation:** First official GitHub collector run (Actions `42346176385`) passed: 14 final NHL games on Nov 5–6, 2026, 1,590 player-period observations, zero per-game collection errors, and explicit per-market source-quarantine counts. The initial historical source files for these two dates predated current `sourceSchemaVersion:5`; therefore hits, faceoff wins, goalie GA and shift TOI had no independent same-game totals and were correctly null-quarantined (rather than falsely declared accurate). A follow-up changes the core official final-stat workflow to prioritize upgrading at most **two recent legacy date files** per run and tags each period archive with the original player-stat schema and fetch timestamp. The period workflow automatically re-collects a date when the canonical official source schema advances, so these legitimate missing values are not permanently frozen as complete. These facts confirm raw official collection, not platform settlement or a validated betting edge.

**Remaining, not complete:** exact platform card metadata and settlement definitions/rights, sufficient historical season-wide period source coverage, period-specific projections and joint combo probabilistic models, prospective committed original-line prediction cohorts and actual result comparisons, role/DNP rules and lineup member collision restrictions. **No combo or period props have been promoted into selectable automatic picks/optimized lineups**, and champion/MLB data remains unchanged. [Dedicated source, reconciliation, model and validation evidence](NHL_COMBO_PERIOD_DEDICATED_DATA_VALIDATION_2026-11-06.md).

### November 6–7 — dedicated period/count and joint-combo modeling (partial)

**Added to code and automatic GitHub period workflow:** `src/nhl-period-count-model.cjs` builds official **period-specific** 1P/2P/3P count projections for SOG, goals, assists, points, blocked shots, hits, faceoffs, saves and goals against from at least eight past *same-period* valid NHL player games. It excludes all games on the evaluation/model-build date and never divides full-game averages by three. `walkForwardPeriodDiagnostics` evaluates fixed 0.5/1.5/2.5 **synthetic diagnostic thresholds**, strictly after eight prior games, with MAE vs prior mean and Brier vs empirical alternatives; these are **not real PrizePicks/Underdog offered-line hit rates**. `src/nhl-period-offer-research.cjs` can derive a conditional experimental Higher/Lower direction only when structured exact player/game/period/quote timestamps, market-specific historical stats, a pre-quote model and participation rule exist. Results remain **not lineup eligible** and original platform quote authentication remains false.

`src/nhl-combo-joint-shadow.cjs` implements two strictly separate **research** probability diagnostics: SAME-game pairs of players require eight actual prior co-game official sums, preserving observed dependence; CROSS-game combos convolve each player's historical count distribution, explicitly labeling the *unverified independence approximation* and requiring eight past values per member. It never invents cross-game co-games or member identities. Both models explicitly return `eligibleForAutoHigherLower:false`, `eligibleForOptimizedLineups:false` until original-source settlement and prospective validation. `scripts/build-nhl-period-combo-models.cjs` writes separate private period forecasts, walk-forward backtest, combo source audit, and period source-offer readiness. Existing 16-market champion, historical MLB and customer website are unchanged.

**Backfill:** the free official NHL event/shift workflow now processes at most **eight completed archived game-dates per scheduled execution** instead of two, making the 21 currently archived 2026–27 dates reachable across several runs. This does NOT assert 2025–26 official period archive coverage; that older season must be backfilled and reconciled separately. No known original-platform complete combo/period feed with structured member identity and settlement is yet available; actual live quote-level validations remain outstanding. Official Underdog [NHL scoring](https://help.underdogsports.com/en/articles/10361760-pick-em-scoring-nhl) defines regulation period validity, player participation and no 3rd-period OT credit; [PrizePicks player pick rules](https://www.prizepicks.com/help-center/player-picks) reference in-app scoring chart and lineup restrictions. We do not assume Underdog's settlement applies to PrizePicks.

**Remaining release gates**: comprehensive last-season and this-season event history and quality thresholds, held-out tests on *actual* platform lines, independent first-party/source-complete combo member and period metadata, observed starting goalie and participation/void rules, platform payout/eligibility constraints, enough prospective Git-committed pregame predictions across many NHL games, and calibration/game-cluster uncertainty. The current Poisson and empirical distributions are deliberately uncalibrated, not verified profitable picks.

### November 6–7 — independent 2025–26 historical period backfill automation (partial)

New `scripts/backfill-nhl-prior-season-periods.cjs` and `.github/workflows/backfill-nhl-prior-season-periods.yml` **isolate prior-season** official NHL schedule, final gamecenter player stats, exact NHL Stats REST supplement counts, play-by-play and shift charts under `outputs/period-stats/training-source/YYYY-MM-DD.json` and `outputs/period-stats/dates/YYYY-MM-DD.json`; they do **not overwrite** `outputs/player-stats`, the six-market champion history/projections, official pregame picks or MLB. The twice-daily free official-source workflow processes at most **five historical 2025–26 dates per run**, records zero-game dates in a separate empty-date checkpoint, and leaves failed/incomplete dates uncommitted for retry. Raw official period results are not platform prop offers.

The standard period workflow subsequently rebuilds the cumulative period history and the new walk-forward period diagnostics across seasons. **The existence of this scheduled workflow does not mean historical backfill is already finished**, that PrizePicks/Underdog original period/combo boards have been authenticated, or that any release-gate has passed. No paid sportsbook requests or wagers.

**2025–26 first live batch recovery:** Run `42348918213` archived **October 7, 2025: 3 completed games, 342 player-period records, 22 source-reconciliation quarantines, 0 official supplement warnings**. October 8 remained incomplete and October 9–11 returned NHL HTTP **429**; none of those four dates was labeled complete. Follow-up introduces **1.2-second minimum gap between all official NHL historical API calls**, honors `Retry-After` with capped exponential 429 retries, and commits `outputs/period-stats/prior-season-backfill-status-latest.json` showing partial failures so a green workflow does not imply a fully completed 2025–26 season. Failed dates remain scheduled for subsequent retries. Existing official current-season collection and production champion are not replaced.

## Phase 4 — Sport-specific projections and calibration

**NHL:** Goals, assists, points, power-play points, SOG, blocks, saves, faceoffs and ice time where source supported; separate skater and goalie models; pace, opponent strength, home/away, injuries, usage, lines, special teams, goalie starts. Count/binary/rate models suited to each stat and period. Model calibration by market and tier.

**NFL:** Passing/rushing/receiving, receptions, TDs, defensive props where available, plus **kicking** (FG attempts/made, extra points and distance-based markets when source data exists); weather, snaps, injuries, scheme and opponent matchups.

**NBA:** Points/rebounds/assists, threes, blocks, steals, turnovers and combinations, minutes, pace, rotation changes and back-to-backs.

**Esports:** CS2, Valorant, League of Legends; game-specific markets, teams/players/rosters, maps, patch versions, map vetoes, tournament format; avoid a shared generic model.

**MLB — LAST:** Preserve the existing collector/backtester and archives unchanged until the preceding sports are ready; then repair grading and model pitchers' strikeouts/outs/earned runs, batter hits/total bases/runs/RBIs and other source-supported props, batting order/park/weather/starting pitchers. Avoid double-running legacy and new systems.

For every sport: score forecasts against both simple baselines and contemporaneous sportsbook-implied probabilities; calibrate before applying percentages as decision thresholds; version and lock models for future outcomes.

**Gate:** No sports-specific probabilities go into the production ranked feed without data coverage, leakage tests, calibration metrics and prospectively reported error bars.

**Design reference:** [Cross-Sport Execution, Survivability & Portfolio Blueprint](CROSS_SPORT_EXECUTION_PORTFOLIO_BLUEPRINT.md), adapted from the user's MLB roadmap PDF. The source's MLB-specific thresholds, historical hit rates, tier policies and coefficients must not be copied into other sports.

**Gate:** Tests verify constraints, correlation, weakest-leg/survivability reporting, exposure and payout behavior. Slip-size and entry-format results are reported separately; claims of optimized EV require verified settlement/payout inputs and prospective held-out performance against simple baselines.

## Phase 10 — Add sports in a controlled order

**2026-11-08 NFL market inventory milestone:** PrizePicks's official NFL guide and 2026 game-page overview, Underdog's official NFL Pick'em settlement rules, and an independently labeled PrizePicks NFL board preview were reviewed without purchasing more provider credits. Built a **115-type NFL source-qualified market registry** with Standard/special line policy gaps, passing/rushing/receiving, defense, touchdown, first-drive, kicking, punting, returns, fantasy, multi-player combos, full game/quarter/half and futures. Every record distinguishes *vendor documentation* from *third-party observations* and rejects unrecognized ambiguous markets and premature automatic picks; documented stat options are **not live original-platform offers**. Implemented complete-input PrizePicks PPR and kicker fantasy/kicking-points research calculations and offline no-paid-call market audit. [NFL source and per-market rollout document](NFL_PRIZEPICKS_UNDERDOG_PROP_CATALOG_2026-11-08.md). The independent public board lists only its top 25 of roughly 48–50 active labels at sampled times, so an exhaustive 2026 market-availability audit **requires a subsequently authorized, complete point-in-time source capture**.

- [x] **[NFL-01]** Source-qualified 115-type NFL prop market inventory and offline unknown-label audit, tested — exact scope is market documentation, canonical IDs, no-loss quarantine, period/token handling and source-versioned PrizePicks fantasy **formula math**, **not** actual new NFL lines, accurate models, settlement, or original-platform access. [NFL inventory test](https://github.com/Thedarkwillow/nhl-betting-platform/actions/runs/42475787944) · [full research suite](https://github.com/Thedarkwillow/nhl-betting-platform/actions/runs/42475771464).
- [ ] **[NFL-02]** Capture all authorized live NFL PrizePicks and Underdog market labels with paginated complete independent/approved sources, exact terms, timestamps, provider provenance, unknowns and per-platform regional availability; enforce API credit budget. **2026-11-25 cross-source reconciliation:** see [shared evidence E1](#cross-source-evidence-index--2026-11-25); existing acceptance gate unchanged.
- [ ] **[NFL-03]** Build official historical NFL player/game event and play-by-play collection, including 2025 and 2026 seasons, snap counts, defensive and special teams data; preserve immutable outcomes and missing-data quarantines. **2026-11-25 cross-source reconciliation:** see [shared evidence E5](#cross-source-evidence-index--2026-11-25); existing acceptance gate unchanged.
- [ ] **[NFL-04]** Validate separate probability models for passing, rushing, receiving, touchdowns, defense and combos using chronological and independently precommitted future tests versus trivial Higher/Lower and sportsbook baselines. **2026-11-25 cross-source reconciliation:** see [shared evidence E5](#cross-source-evidence-index--2026-11-25); existing acceptance gate unchanged.
- [ ] **[NFL-05]** Build and validate dedicated kicking/punting models with per-attempt FG distance, opportunities, XP, special teams, venue/weather, drive context and market-specific void policies; validate user-visible fantasy rules against current platform. **2026-11-25 cross-source reconciliation:** see [shared evidence E5](#cross-source-evidence-index--2026-11-25); existing acceptance gate unchanged.
- [ ] **[NFL-06]** Freeze exact pregame NFL model choices and grade official finals prospectively, with platform-specific payout/settlement and correlation-aware 2–6 pick optimization gates; no wager placement.
- [ ] **[NFL-07]** Add NFL to our mobile sport-first website with reliable source freshness, player/market/period/tier filters, research-only predictions, completeness alerts and historical scorecard. **2026-11-25 cross-source reconciliation:** see [shared evidence E7](#cross-source-evidence-index--2026-11-25); existing acceptance gate unchanged.
- [ ] **[NFL-08]** Add isolated model+settlement validation for exotic quarter/half, drive, first-to-X, returns, punt, multi-player and leader markets; never infer these grades from ordinary full-game totals or automatically promote unsupported variants.



1. **NHL FIRST** — finish validation, goalie, archived markets and production-quality UX.
2. **NFL SECOND, including kicking** — football-specific data and models.
3. **NBA THIRD** — minutes, usage, lineup injuries, pace and on/off context.
4. **ESPORTS FOURTH** — CS2 → Valorant → League of Legends, gated by usable historical match/prop coverage.
5. **MLB LAST** — preserve existing repository and archived data now; only after preceding sports launch, audit/repair legacy grading and integrate MLB into the shared website.

**This is a firm project-priority order.** Do not proactively run MLB repair/migration tasks earlier unless needed to prevent data loss or address a security issue. Never use one generic cross-sport probability formula.

- [ ] **[SHARP-03]** Multi-line **alternate ladder visualization**: show Standard, alternative thresholds, implied probability curve, provider-indicated Goblin/Demon candidates, data gaps and invalid price-to-payout assumptions. **2026-11-25 cross-source reconciliation:** see [shared evidence E2](#cross-source-evidence-index--2026-11-25); existing acceptance gate unchanged.
- [ ] **[SHARP-04]** **What changed?** explainable movement diff: opening/current/close, added/removed/suspended/reposted, tier change, provider, timestamp, max quote age and model effect; support replay by game/date. **2026-11-25 cross-source reconciliation:** see [shared evidence E6](#cross-source-evidence-index--2026-11-25); existing acceptance gate unchanged.

- [ ] **[SHARP-07]** Opponent/matchup engine beyond last-10 trends: rolling usage, opponent style, pace, special-teams opportunity, rest/travel, home/away, line deployment and score-state sensitivity; use out-of-sample checks. **2026-11-25 cross-source reconciliation:** see [shared evidence E4](#cross-source-evidence-index--2026-11-25); existing acceptance gate unchanged.
- [ ] **[SHARP-08]** Interactive player prop detail: last 5/10/20, seasonal splits, opponent history, usage, minutes/TOI, distribution histogram, observed vs projected, model drivers and confidence intervals; show selection bias for tiny samples. **2026-11-25 cross-source reconciliation:** see [shared evidence E4](#cross-source-evidence-index--2026-11-25); existing acceptance gate unchanged.

## Phase 15 — Subscription, access control and commercial economics (P0 for selling)

- [ ] **[BIZ-01]** Written policy/legal review confirming each upstream feed permits paid user-facing commercial analytics, caching, derivative display, public source attribution and projected audience; **do not resell raw odds streams**. The Odds API's specific terms are only one part of this audit. **2026-11-25 cross-source reconciliation:** see [shared evidence E8](#cross-source-evidence-index--2026-11-25); existing acceptance gate unchanged.
- [ ] **[BIZ-02]** Determine jurisdiction-specific sports-research publication, ad/affiliate, age and gambling-promotion obligations with qualified review. No wagers or implicit licensed-operator claims.
- [ ] **[BIZ-03]** Select a payments provider **only after confirming underwriting/approval for this betting-adjacent analytics model**. Never assume Stripe or another vendor automatically supports it.
- [ ] **[BIZ-04]** Define tested **Free / Research Pro / Power Research** plan entitlements using feature access, retention or alert limits—not promises of winners; validate pricing via beta interviews.
- [ ] **[BIZ-05]** Secure billing lifecycle (checkout, subscription renewals, taxes where applicable, cancellations, refunds, payment-failed handling), signed webhooks, idempotent events, sandbox tests and receipts.
- [ ] **[BIZ-06]** Server-side entitlement enforcement on **every paid route/API query** with RBAC, revocations, trials, rate limits and audit logs. Client UI must not be the paywall.
- [ ] **[BIZ-07]** Clear subscription terms: renewal frequency, amount, trial conversion, affirmative consent, easy cancellation, refund and support policies; legal review of applicable federal/state consumer law.
- [ ] **[BIZ-08]** Business unit economics dashboard: monthly recurring revenue (MRR), ARR, churn, retention cohorts, customer acquisition cost (CAC), lifetime value (LTV), gross margin and **provider credit cost per active user**.
- [ ] **[BIZ-09]** Pricing and go-to-market experiment: small free tier + invitation-only beta, collect useful feature feedback, measure engagement/retention before paid launch, avoid manipulative scarcity.
- [ ] **[BIZ-10]** Honest promotional claims review, consent-first email, affiliate sponsorship disclosures if ever used, no cherry-picked ROI or win-rate advertising.
- [ ] **[BIZ-11]** Design billing/metering for separate sport and premium feature entitlements without forcing MLB development before its last-phase slot.
- [ ] **[BIZ-12]** Prelaunch smoke test: signup → verify age/access → free tier → approved payment → upgraded access → refund/cancel → data deletion request → access revoked.

**Gate:** Written source-rights and merchant eligibility confirmed, legal/compliance reviewed, subscriber authorization and cancellation verified. **Do not charge before this gate.**

## Phase 16 — Institutional reliability, security and privacy (P0)

- [ ] **[OPS-01]** Central immutable raw-event storage, typed normalized schemas, idempotent ETL, source-specific coverage manifest and reproducible derived research. **2026-11-25 cross-source reconciliation:** see [shared evidence E3](#cross-source-evidence-index--2026-11-25); existing acceptance gate unchanged.
- [ ] **[OPS-02]** Account-wide API credit governor shared across all The Odds API workflows, with emergency stop, projections of quota exhaustion, daily cost reports and alerting; existing separate caps are insufficient.
- [ ] **[OPS-03]** Production observability: job success/failure, collector freshness, dataset completeness, provider API errors, normalized market join quality, feature drift, model calibration and stale-line rates. **2026-11-25 cross-source reconciliation:** see [shared evidence E3](#cross-source-evidence-index--2026-11-25); existing acceptance gate unchanged.
- [ ] **[OPS-04]** High-availability app design: queues, bounded concurrency, cache TTL, retries/backoff, backup and recovery drills, staged migrations and infrastructure-as-code. **2026-11-25 cross-source reconciliation:** see [shared evidence E3](#cross-source-evidence-index--2026-11-25); existing acceptance gate unchanged.
- [ ] **[OPS-05]** Security hardening aligned to OWASP: secure authentication, per-object authorization, secrets rotation, WAF/rate limits, SSRF safety, dependency scans, secure webhooks and abuse/fraud controls.
- [ ] **[OPS-06]** Data protection: minimize personal data, encryption, access controls, retention/deletion, export access, incident response and privacy policy aligned to actual operation.
- [ ] **[OPS-07]** Staging and production separation with automated unit/integration/e2e/mobile smoke and deployment rollback; no testing that spends paid Odds API credits unless budgeted. **2026-11-25 cross-source reconciliation:** see [shared evidence E3](#cross-source-evidence-index--2026-11-25); existing acceptance gate unchanged.
- [ ] **[OPS-08]** Defined SLOs **as targets**, not current performance: e.g. >99.5% site availability, <5 minute delay for *supported frequent-update* sources where contract permits, and <1% invalid joins; measure and adjust from real provider capabilities.
- [ ] **[OPS-09]** Secure admin console (not public) to diagnose providers, suspend bad market types, inspect logs without exposing secrets, and manually approve public performance claims.
- [x] **[OPS-10]** Document browser/mobile experience, source and market degradation, offline/stale display and alert outage fallbacks.

**Gate:** Security review, restore drill, access test, representative peak-load test and audited incident-response path pass before subscriptions launch.

## Phase 17 — Trust, transparency, responsible use and customer assurance (P0)

- [ ] **[TRUST-01]** Every ranked recommendation shows model confidence, calibration status, sample size, source freshness, unverified fields, correlated risk and no-bet conditions. **2026-11-25 cross-source reconciliation:** see [shared evidence E7](#cross-source-evidence-index--2026-11-25); existing acceptance gate unchanged.
- [ ] **[TRUST-02]** Clearly separate **estimated probabilities**, retrospective hit rates, actual prospectively recorded hit rates, sportsbook fair-market views and verified platform settlements. **2026-11-25 cross-source reconciliation:** see [shared evidence E7](#cross-source-evidence-index--2026-11-25); existing acceptance gate unchanged.
- [ ] **[TRUST-03]** Display model-versioned **all-pick public ledger**, performance by date/sport/stat and intervals, plus corrections/changelog; prevent cherry-picking. **2026-11-25 cross-source reconciliation:** see [shared evidence E7](#cross-source-evidence-index--2026-11-25); existing acceptance gate unchanged.
- [ ] **[TRUST-04]** Responsible-gambling messaging, configurable alert suppression and session/notification limits, age/jurisdiction gating based on qualified review; no pressure-to-wager engagement loops.
- [ ] **[TRUST-05]** Independent quality audit/pilot with measured false-positive rates, missing-data handling, payout decision tests, source terms and refund/security controls before claiming "sharp".
- [ ] **[TRUST-06]** Accessibility and transparent customer support: status incident banner, report-a-bug, plain-English data limitations and escalation route.

**Gate:** Public claims align with reproducible source/prospective evidence; subscriber expectations and constraints are clear.

## Phase 18 — Market launch, retention, brand and profitability research (P1/P2)

- [ ] **[GROW-01]** Identify the initial niche (recommended NHL-first) and 20–30 potential beta users; document their research pain points and willingness to pay rather than choosing prices by guesswork.
- [ ] **[GROW-02]** Build an SEO-readable **public educational research library** about stat definitions, sample sizes, calibration, market mechanics, source limitations and model reports, with original commentary.
- [ ] **[GROW-03]** Honest product landing page, transparent pricing/feature comparisons, release notes, uptime and public results dashboard. No claims of "guaranteed picks", high win percentages without evidence, or fabricated testimonials.
- [ ] **[GROW-04]** Consent-based referral or affiliate programs only with contract review and clear FTC-style disclosures; defer until users value the core model.
- [ ] **[GROW-05]** Privacy-respecting product analytics (signup-to-value funnel, 7/30 day retention, features used, alerts engaged) and structured feedback; avoid manipulative engagement loops.
- [ ] **[GROW-06]** Customer support SOPs, issue priorities, refund/cancellation playbooks and public source incident notices.
- [ ] **[GROW-07]** Plan staged access: internal QA → **free private beta** → validated premium pilot → controlled paid launch; explicit go/no-go decision at each step.
- [ ] **[GROW-08]** Business readiness gate: positive expected unit economics after data licensing/API, hosting, processing, support and acquisition costs, with refunds/chargebacks stress test.

**Gate:** Demonstrated user value, sustainable data costs and launch-compliant operation—not merely a website with a paywall.

No licensed forward/PP/shot-quality ingestion. Snapshot season-to-date after earlier games, so NO retrospective replay or claimed edge. Await genuine future scoring. No model promotion until enough independent official-finished games. **Additional partial 2026-11-02:** passing MoneyPuck game-level, prior-date-only skater xOnGoal/xGoals ablation on 2024–25/2025–26 history. 46,459 held-out 2025–26 comparisons per market over 1,312 games and 167 dates: SOG MAE weighted-form 1.051958 versus shot-quality blend 1.047207 (tiny improvement), goals 0.269057 versus 0.270069 (regression). ## Phase 20 — Extract the strongest lessons from our Betting project chats

**Sources:** The project conversation and the prior chat summaries available in this session. See the [conversation-to-roadmap gap analysis](PROJECT_CHATS_RESEARCH_GAPS.md) for what was already covered versus what needed an explicit test. This is **not** a claim that every archived private chat is searchable. Implement shared infrastructure **NHL first**; MLB stays **last**.

- [ ] **[CHAT-01]** **P0 Full prop lifecycle observer:** Capture first-seen, repriced, moved, re-tiered, suspended/removed, reposted and locked offers; mark unobserved intervals UNKNOWN. Gate: fixture prevents withdrawn or stale lines appearing as available. **2026-11-25 cross-source reconciliation:** see [shared evidence E6](#cross-source-evidence-index--2026-11-25); existing acceptance gate unchanged.
- [ ] **[CHAT-02]** **P0 Original tier verification test bench:** Compare same-game Standard and alternate lines against authenticated Standard/Goblin/Demon labels where present; keep +100/-137 only as provider-indicated candidates and multiplier separate. Gate: multiple alternatives and contradictions rejected. **2026-11-25 cross-source reconciliation:** see [shared evidence E2](#cross-source-evidence-index--2026-11-25); existing acceptance gate unchanged.
- [ ] **[CHAT-03]** **P0 Historical source-provenance ladder:** Keep first-party offers, third-party-labelled archived quotes, dated editorial records and statistical reconstructions in different evidence classes. Gate: schema cannot promote low-quality evidence to original board or invent line movements. **2026-11-25 cross-source reconciliation:** see [shared evidence E8](#cross-source-evidence-index--2026-11-25); existing acceptance gate unchanged.
- [ ] **[CHAT-04]** **P0 2025–26 historical coverage map:** Show date × event × market × standard/alternate × source/tier availability and explicit unqueried, unavailable, missing and unknown states with credits per usable record. Gate: reports distinguish missing offers from absent data.
- [ ] **[CHAT-05]** **P0 Exact-line sportsbook agreement:** Compare identical player/game/stat/period/side and synchronized quote time; identify nearest-line versus exact match, vig removal and source disagreements. Gate: reject mismatches and false positive market edges. **2026-11-25 cross-source reconciliation:** see [shared evidence E2](#cross-source-evidence-index--2026-11-25); existing acceptance gate unchanged.
- [ ] **[CHAT-06]** **P0 NHL vendor-to-official-stat conformance fixtures:** Test provider keys for goals versus points, goal-plus-assist totals, saves, blocks, power play and period/full-game; account for NHL corrections versus original platform settlement and ambiguous player IDs. Gate: grader coverage tests by market and scoring version.

Gate: immutable evidence and prospective grading without hindsight selection. **2026-11-25 cross-source reconciliation:** see [shared evidence E5](#cross-source-evidence-index--2026-11-25); existing acceptance gate unchanged.

Each must be implemented in our NHL/shared system, have representative output, a passing automated test, and a dated entry in the [Verified completion log](#verified-completion-log--mark-tasks-here-as-they-are-actually-finished) before being marked done. Provider-indicated odds, independent archives and retrospective backtests are not authenticated original PrizePicks tier/payout or demonstrated prospective accuracy. **MLB is last.**

## Phase 21 — Verified NHL implementation milestones, November 2026

These are tightly scoped completed implementation milestones. They do **not** mean prospective prediction accuracy, commercial source rights, true PrizePicks settlement, payout-adjusted expected value, or the whole mobile product are production ready.

- [x] **[NHLQ-01]** **Recover and audit archival NHL game identities (scoped).** Resolves 1,180 previously unknown game IDs from immutable original team/opponent/event-time evidence and exact cached NHL schedule; quarantines 64 records with no original opponent/start metadata rather than guessing. Verified by automated `nhl-archived-game-identity` tests and post-run `outputs/grading/nhl-latest.json`. A complete cross-provider identity system remains outstanding (`MLBX-16`, `CHAT-06`).
- [x] **[NHLQ-02]** **Prospective pregame outcome readiness audit (scoped).** Separates upcoming, nonfinal, official-final-without-player-stats and unknown-status predictions; reports baseline accuracy only for actually graded pregame records. Verified initial cohort: 621 eligible pending across 4 official NHL games, all not yet started at grading time, 0 completed eligible outcomes, no invented hit rate. Grader/test CI passed; full prospective accuracy (`EDGE-01`, `EDGE-04`) remains outstanding.
- [x] **[NHLQ-03]** **NHL public research dashboard visibility + data-safety (scoped).** Published sanitized pregame identity/accuracy readiness, 2025–26 historical Standard/alternate matching and goalie saves summary to the public data repo; page includes accessible mobile display and suppresses stale live lineup suggestions. Verified using dedicated public dashboard source validation and successful backend sanitized publisher. This is **not** a paid multi-sport website, or verified live PrizePicks offers.
- [x] **[NHLQ-04]** **First true pregame completed-game grading acceptance (scoped).** Accepted 2026-11-02: 22 archived pregame NHL props graded against official Nov 1 completed-game player boxscores, **20 decided (10 correct = 50.0%), 2 pushes**; always Lower 55.0%, always Higher 45.0%. First Git-commit archive time + source capture + pregame forecast timestamps verified; missing-player, missing-stat and Push guards tested. [Successful grader CI](https://github.com/Thedarkwillow/nhl-betting-platform/actions/runs/41553046346) and [64-exception source audit](NHL_64_IDENTITY_EXCEPTION_AUDIT_2026-11-02.md). **Not** verified first-party platform settlements, a profitable edge or statistically adequate calibration; broader P03-04/EDGE-04 remains open.
- [x] **[NHLQ-05]** **Deployed website HTTP smoke test (scoped).** GitHub Actions retrieved live Pages HTML and published `data.json` over HTTPS, verified the new research sections, validated absence of unverified payout/accuracy claims, and confirmed stale-lineup suppression code. [Deployed smoke CI passed](https://github.com/Thedarkwillow/nhl-betting-platform1/actions/runs/41549650202). **Manual mobile browser/device UX testing remains open in the broader website phase.**

**Next acceptance priority:** `CHAT-06` official-stat conformance, `EDGE-04` multi-day prospective comparison and uncertainty, `P03-05` true platform settlement/DNP tracking and `OPS-02` comprehensive job/credit health. Both `NHLQ-04` and `NHLQ-05` have now passed their limited-scope acceptance gates. Existing current NHL and historical collectors remain independent; MLB stays **last**.

## The very next work session (Monday, Nov 2, after 4 PM Pacific)

1. **Audit overnight GitHub runs**, examine credit usage, market extraction success, stale/missing source rows and test failures; fix as necessary.
2. **Check Standard/alternate match quality**: validate per-event player+stat+side+snapshot matches; investigate why alternates are unmatched; test goalie saves first.
3. **Reassess spend:** both collectors together have separate 3,000-credit cumulative ceilings. Add global shared monthly cap and stop criteria before scaling backfill.
4. **Repair and populate prospective NHL outcomes**: 0 verified graded / 431 pending at last inspected snapshot. Determine which are actually eligible, archive original pregame timestamps, and grade completed games without DNP confusion.
5. **Prioritize model improvement** using 2025–26 reconstructed result ~49.95% vs ~51.46% always Lower: calibrate, test stat-specific baselines and freeze a prospective candidate.
6. **Push the next production website slice:** surface historical coverage, market matches and data-quality statuses on NHL page, without calling provider-inferred tiers verified.
7. **Start the commercial foundation without charging users:** verify data rights, design the subscriber entitlement model, and build a read-only performance/quality dashboard. **MLB repairs and expansion are deferred to LAST**; preserve its current archives.

## Phase 23 — PrizePicks and Underdog long-term sport expansion (owner-approved backlog, 2026-11-25)

Keep Phase 10's original sport rollout order intact. Complete current PickFinder authentication/capture, model qualification and prospective evidence before expanding production coverage. Current shared registry includes 14 categories; five are registration-only/partial (WNBA, CFB, CBB, tennis, soccer). The preceding coverage audit identified 16 additional categories with reported independent-provider offer history and five further reported categories requiring individual offer verification. This is not a verified complete live PrizePicks inventory, nor proof that Underdog carries the same sports. Soccer league subdivisions below are not counted as additional sports. The source catalog is a discovery lead, not authorization or original-platform proof. Maintain season awareness and the no-new-paid-credit policy across every category.

Expansion order after existing rollout: related sport families (WNBA, CBB, WCBB, CFB, CFL, KBO); additional esports (R6, APEX, Rocket League pending verification); individual sports (tennis, UFC, PGA, European golf, boxing); soccer subdivisions, F1, NASCAR, cricket; specialty sports (TT, badminton, darts, WTGL) and other verified categories. Priority is flexible based on actual competition, source quality, existing infrastructure and owner approval. Do not start collection or promote any model solely because it is on this list.

Use existing authorized Premium and documented free metadata first; distinguish missing, disabled, offseason, partial, and source failure; do not equate catalog entries with live offers.
